 203 |   const getAllowedZoneForEvent = (event: ReactDragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
 204 |     const zone = getZoneForEvent(event);
 205 |     if (!zone) return null;
 206 |     const payload = parseThreadDragPayload(event);
 207 |     if (
 208 |       payload &&
 209 |       !isThreadDragPayloadAllowed(payload, {

 213 |       return null;

 230 |     if (!isThreadDrag(event)) return;

 238 |   const handleDragLeave = (event: ReactDragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
 239 |     if (!isThreadDrag(event)) return;
 240 |     const wrapper = wrapperRef.current;
 241 |     if (!wrapper) return;
 242 |     const related = event.relatedTarget as Node | null;
 243 |     if (related && wrapper.contains(related)) return;
 244 |     resetOverlayState();
 245 |   };
 246 | 
 247 |   const handleDrop = (event: ReactDragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
 248 |     if (!isThreadDrag(event)) return;
 249 |     event.preventDefault();
 250 |     event.stopPropagation();

 254 |     resetOverlayState();
 255 | 
 256 |     if (!zone || !payload) return;

 258 |     const { direction, side } = dropZoneToDirectionSide(zone);
 259 |     onDrop({ ...payload, direction, side });
 260 |   };
 261 | 
 262 |   useEffect(() => {
 263 |     resetOverlayState();
 264 |     return resetOverlayState;
